- **tongjian-decision-trace**: 当用户面对一项**已经发生或正在执行的举措**（自己下的、别人下的、或刚被推到自己头上的）， 想判断它动了什么秩序、触了谁的利益、多久之后会反噬、以及下一步该撤该改该留时使用。 把《資治通鑑》的编年叙事与「臣光曰」史论收成一套可读的追蹤方法：名分线（授出了什么只能由某个位置授出的东西）、 人线（谁得谁失、得利者是否同时握有反噬能力）、时滞线（反噬走哪条通路、多久到），最后落到三选一处置。 适用：组织内的破格提拔 / 例外放行 / 职权重划 / 承诺兑现或透支 / 换人换将 / 对外让步求和，以及读完一条坏消息想判断它会不会连到自己。 不做：预测吉凶、算命、给历史人物翻案、把史事当寓言朗诵、用一句话点评天下大势。 不用于：还没有任何举措、正要开局的立项评估（用 five-affairs-seven-questions）；或想判断某个人靠不靠谱（用 lunyu-conduct）。 ([yihexiang/rulai-distill](https://github.com/yihexiang/rulai-distill/tree/HEAD/examples/open-bundle/skills/tongjian-decision-trace) · ★2)

- **tool-design-zh**: 给 Agent 设计工具、写 Agent 能据以路由的工具描述、工具 schema 与响应格式、参数命名规范、可自纠错的错误信息、MCP 服务器设计与命名、工具集合并与瘦身、决定该加还是删一个工具、架构减法（用少量原语工具替代一堆专用工具）。当用户说「Agent 老是选错工具」「怎么写工具描述」「我们有 17 个工具太乱了」「MCP 工具名怎么起」「错误信息怎么写 Agent 才会自己改」「要不要把这些工具合并」时使用。核心：工具是确定性系统与非确定性 Agent 之间的契约，描述必须单独就把契约说清；合并原则——人类工程师说不清该用哪个的，Agent 也说不清；描述回答四问（做什么/何时用/输入什么/返回什么）；动词-名词命名、跨工具一致；错误信息面向 Agent 恢复；响应提供 concise/detailed；MCP 用 ServerName:tool_name 全限定名。附最佳实践与架构减法案例参考。改编自 Agent Skills for Context Engineering 的 tool-design（MIT）。 ([po-et/workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/po-et/workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/ported/skills/tool-design-zh) · ★4)

- **tool-die-maker**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a Tool and Die Maker — compensating a stamping die for material springback before cutting the forming angle, setting punch-to-die clearance for a given material and thickness, scaling an injection mold cavity for resin shrinkage, sequencing progressive die stations against carrier-web integrity, or re-verifying critical dimensions after heat treatment before final fitting. - **tool-diet**: You are auditing the current session for avoidable token/cost waste from tool usage. Your job is to interrupt bash-heavy browsing before it turns into a bloated context window. ([chokevin/kstack](https://github.com/chokevin/kstack/tree/HEAD/skills/tool-diet) · ★1)
- **tool-diet**: Put tool results on a diet. Measure which tool, CLI, script or MCP results in a session cost the most tokens for the least use, cut the fat at its source, and prove the saving. Use when a tool result floods the context, the same lookup keeps repeating, a CLI or MCP server returns far more than the task used, or the user asks why a session burned tokens. Not for shortening the agent's own replies. ([stevesolun/steveskills](https://github.com/stevesolun/steveskills/tree/HEAD/tool-diet))
- **tool-difc-courts-search**: Use when searching DIFC Courts case law or checking case status for matters before the Dubai International Financial Centre Courts — an English common-law court in Dubai's financial free zone. Covers Court of First Instance, Court of Appeal, Small Claims Tribunal, and Wills & Probate Registry. Returns party names, case numbers, judge, judgment PDFs. Note the DIFC-LCIA arbitration centre was replaced by DIAC in 2021. Pair with ADGM courts search for forum-shopping analysis.
